The **Volkswagen Golf (Mk8)** (also known as the Golf VIII) is a
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Compact_car" id="mwHg"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Compact car">compact car</a>, the eighth
generation of the
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Volkswagen_Golf" id="mwHw"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Volkswagen Golf">Volkswagen Golf</a> and the
successor to the
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Volkswagen_Golf_Mk7" id="mwIA"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Volkswagen Golf Mk7">Volkswagen Golf Mk7</a>.<sup><a href="#cite_note-6" id="mwIQ"><span id="mwIg"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwIw"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>6<span id="mwJA"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup> It was launched in
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wolfsburg" id="mwJQ"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Wolfsburg">Wolfsburg</a> on 24 October 2019,
and arrived in German showrooms in December
2019.<sup><a href="#cite_note-7" id="mwJg"><span id="mwJw"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwKA"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>7<span id="mwKQ"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup>

The Golf Mk8 uses the same
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Volkswagen_Group_MQB_platform"
id="mwKw" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Volkswagen Group MQB platform">MQB
Evo platform</a> as the fourth-generation
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Audi_A3" id="mwLA"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Audi A3">Audi A3</a> and
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SEAT_León" id="mwLQ"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="SEAT León">SEAT León</a>.<sup><a href="#cite_note-8" id="mwLg"><span id="mwLw"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwMA"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>8<span id="mwMQ"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup>

</div>

<div id="mwMw" class="section" mw-section-id="1"
aria-labelledby="Features">

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2">

## Features

<span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\[</span>[edit](/w/index.php?title=Volkswagen_Golf_Mk8&action=edit&section=1 "Edit section: Features")<span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\]</span></span>

</div>

The exterior of the Mk8 has evolutionary design changes, with a new
two-dimensional Volkswagen logo and more angular styling. At the rear
there are new L-shaped taillights similar to those of the T-Roc. A more
prominent downward arch at the nose contributes to a reduced
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Automobile_drag_coefficient"
id="mwNQ" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Automobile drag coefficient">drag
coefficient</a>; the Mk8's is
*C*<span class="small">*d*</span><span about="#mwt44">=0.275</span>
compared to the Mk7's
*C*<span class="small">*d*</span><span about="#mwt45">=0.29</span>,
giving a drag area of 0.61
m<sup>2</sup>.<sup><a href="#cite_note-9" id="mwOQ"><span id="mwOg"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwOw"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>9<span id="mwPA"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a><a href="#cite_note-10" id="mwPQ"><span id="mwPg"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwPw"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>10<span id="mwQA"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup> The dimensions are
roughly the same as the Mk7, 29<span id="mwQQ"
typeof="mw:Entity"> </span>mm longer, and 10<span id="mwQg"
typeof="mw:Entity"> </span>mm narrower with a similar wheelbase. The
three-door hatchback has been discontinued, leaving the five-door
hatchback as the only model available at launch. The Golf Estate
(Variant) was revealed in September 2020, alongside a rugged "Alltrack"
version with slightly raised suspension, body cladding, and
all-wheel-drive.<sup><a href="#cite_note-11" id="mwQw"><span id="mwRA"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwRQ"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>11<span id="mwRg"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup>

<div id="mwRw" class="section" mw-section-id="2"
aria-labelledby="Technology">

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3">

### Technology

<span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\[</span>[edit](/w/index.php?title=Volkswagen_Golf_Mk8&action=edit&section=2 "Edit section: Technology")<span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\]</span></span>

</div>

The Mk8 features several technological
advancements,<sup><a href="#cite_note-12" id="mwSQ"><span id="mwSg"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwSw"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>12<span id="mwTA"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup> including standard LED
headlights on all models and optional matrix LEDs, an advanced head-up
display, a
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shift_by_wire" id="mwTQ"
class="mw-redirect" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Shift by wire">shift by
wire</a> system on models with the dual-clutch gearbox,
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Amazon_Alexa" id="mwTg"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Amazon Alexa">Alexa</a> integration, and an
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Near-field_communication"
id="mwTw" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Near-field communication">NFC</a>
mobile key with compatible Samsung smartphones via
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ESIM" id="mwUA" rel="mw:WikiLink"
title="ESIM">eSIM</a>. The interior received a major overhaul, replacing
the traditional analog instrument panel with an entirely digital 10.25"
driver's display and either an 8.25" or 10" center
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Touchscreen" id="mwUQ"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Touchscreen">touchscreen</a> for multimedia.
Instead of physical buttons, a touch-sensitive panel houses controls for
various functions, including the headlights and air
conditioning/ventilation. The vehicle can also be upgraded with new
functions after purchasing it, such as adaptive cruise control, WiFi
hotspot, online-based voice control, Light Assist, navigation and
Wireless
App-Connect.<sup><span typeof="mw:Entity">\[</span>*<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span
title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (May 2022)">citation
needed</span></a>*<span typeof="mw:Entity">\]</span></sup> The reliance
on touchscreen controls in place of conventional physical buttons,
knobs, or dials was criticised as being frustrating and unintuitive to
use.<sup><a href="#cite_note-13" id="mwVA"><span id="mwVQ"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwVg"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>13<span id="mwVw"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a><a href="#cite_note-14" id="mwWA"><span id="mwWQ"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwWg"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>14<span id="mwWw"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup>
*<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Which%3F" id="mwXQ"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Which?">Which?</a>* noted that "too much
attention must be diverted from the road to operate simple functions
such as the climate control" and called the layout "a step
backwards."<sup><a href="#cite_note-15" id="mwXg"><span id="mwXw"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwYA"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>15<span id="mwYQ"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a><a href="#cite_note-16" id="mwYg"><span id="mwYw"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwZA"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>16<span id="mwZQ"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup>

<div id="mwZg" class="section" mw-section-id="3"
aria-labelledby="Assistance_systems">

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4">

#### Assistance systems

<span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\[</span>[edit](/w/index.php?title=Volkswagen_Golf_Mk8&action=edit&section=3 "Edit section: Assistance systems")<span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\]</span></span>

</div>

The Mk8 includes a <a
href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Self-driving_car#Commercialization"
id="mwaA" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Self-driving car">semi-autonomous</a>
driving system known as Travel Assist that can work at speeds of up to
<span id="mwaQ" about="#mwt71" typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;210&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;kph&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;mph&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">210</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt71"> </span><span about="#mwt71">km/h
(130</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt71"> </span><span about="#mwt71">mph)</span> by using the
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Adaptive_cruise_control"
id="mwag" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Adaptive cruise control">adaptive
cruise control</a> and
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lane_departure_warning_system"
id="mwaw" rel="mw:WikiLink"
title="Lane departure warning system">lane-keeping assist systems</a>.<sup><a href="#cite_note-17" id="mwbA"><span id="mwbQ"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwbg"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>17<span id="mwbw"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup>
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Capacitive_sensing" id="mwcA"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Capacitive sensing">Capacitive sensors</a> in
the steering wheel detect when the driver is touching it; if no input is
detected for more than 15 seconds, the system will warn the driver
before
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Emergency_driver_assistant"
id="mwcQ" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Emergency driver assistant">Emergency
Assist</a> is activated and the vehicle is brought to a safe stop. It is
also the first Volkswagen vehicle to use
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vehicle-to-everything" id="mwcg"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Vehicle-to-everything">Car2X</a>, where
information can be exchanged with other cars and the traffic
infrastructure within a radius of up to <span id="mwcw" about="#mwt72"
typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;800&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;m&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;ft&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">800</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt72"> </span><span about="#mwt72">m
(2,625</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt72"> </span><span about="#mwt72">ft)</span>.<sup><a href="#cite_note-18" id="mwdA"><span id="mwdQ"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwdg"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>18<span id="mwdw"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup>

- <div id="mweQ">

  <div id="mweg" class="thumb" style="width: 230px; height: 158px;">

  <span id="mwew" typeof="mw:File"><a

<div id="mwrw" class="section" mw-section-id="4"
aria-labelledby="Golf_GTE">

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2">

## Golf GTE

<span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\[</span>[edit](/w/index.php?title=Volkswagen_Golf_Mk8&action=edit&section=4 "Edit section: Golf GTE")<span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\]</span></span>

</div>

The Golf GTE is a
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hot_hatchback" id="mwsQ"
class="mw-redirect" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Hot hatchback">hot
hatchback</a> version with a
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Plug-in_hybrid" id="mwsg"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Plug-in hybrid">plug-in hybrid</a> drivetrain
that produces <span id="mwsw" about="#mwt80" typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;180&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;kW&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;PS kW hp&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;order&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;out&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">245</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt80"> </span><span about="#mwt80">PS
(180</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt80"> </span><span about="#mwt80">kW;
241</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt80"> </span><span about="#mwt80">hp)</span>. It has an
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/All-electric_range" id="mwtA"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="All-electric range">all-electric range</a> of
about <span id="mwtQ" about="#mwt81" typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;60&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;km&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;mi&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;off&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">60
kilometres (37 miles)</span> in EV mode, with a 13
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kilowatt_hour" id="mwtg"
class="mw-redirect" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Kilowatt hour">kWh</a>
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lithium_ion_battery" id="mwtw"
class="mw-redirect" rel="mw:WikiLink"
title="Lithium ion battery">lithium ion battery</a> supplementing the
1.4-litre TSI direct-injection
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Petrol_engine" id="mwuA"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Petrol engine">petrol engine</a>. The GTE, GTD,
and GTI have different styling to distinguish them from less powerful
Golf models. The front has a large honeycomb grille with a blue accent
and an
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Light-emitting_diode" id="mwuQ"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Light-emitting diode">LED</a> light strip
positioned below, a black lower bumper trim, and an integrated
background exterior light. The rear has a
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diffuser_(automotive)" id="mwug"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Diffuser (automotive)">diffuser</a>-style rear
bumper. The roof
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spoiler_(car)" id="mwuw"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Spoiler (car)">spoiler</a> is also different,
with wider side sill panels, larger wheels, and red brake calipers.
Inside, there are many minor differences, including sports seats with
different interior fabrics and other minor changes.
<sup><a href="#cite_note-19" id="mwvA"><span id="mwvQ"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwvg"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>19<span id="mwvw"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a><a href="#cite_note-Motor1_GTD/GTE-20" id="mwwA"><span id="mwwQ"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwwg"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>20<span id="mwww"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup>

The new 2024 facelift includes a larger 1.5-litre
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Turbocharger" id="mwxQ"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Turbocharger">TSI</a>
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Direct_Injection_Spark_Ignition"
id="mwxg" class="mw-redirect" rel="mw:WikiLink"
title="Direct Injection Spark Ignition">direct-injection</a> petrol
engine and a bigger 19.7 kWh battery. Like the pre-facelift it has a
standard Golf engine but with a 95 PS (70 kW; 93 hp)
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Electric_motor" id="mwxw"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Electric motor">electric motor</a> that sits in
between the
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Internal_combustion_engine"
id="mwyA" rel="mw:WikiLink"
title="Internal combustion engine">engine</a>
(<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Flywheel" id="mwyQ"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Flywheel">flywheel</a>) and
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Direct-shift_gearbox" id="mwyg"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Direct-shift gearbox">DSG Gearbox.</a>

<div id="mw3g" class="section" mw-section-id="5"
aria-labelledby="Golf_GTD">

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2">

## Golf GTD

<span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\[</span>[edit](/w/index.php?title=Volkswagen_Golf_Mk8&action=edit&section=5 "Edit section: Golf GTD")<span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\]</span></span>

</div>

The Golf GTD is a
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Turbo-diesel" id="mw4A"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Turbo-diesel">turbodiesel</a> hot-hatchback
version that is powered by a 2.0-litre
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Turbocharger" id="mw4Q"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Turbocharger">turbocharged</a> direct-injection
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diesel_engine" id="mw4g"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Diesel engine">diesel engine</a> (TDI)
producing <span id="mw4w" about="#mwt89" typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;147&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;kW&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;PS kW hp&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;order&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;out&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">200</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt89"> </span><span about="#mwt89">PS
(147</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt89"> </span><span about="#mwt89">kW;
197</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt89"> </span><span about="#mwt89">hp)</span> and
<span id="mw5A" about="#mwt90" typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;400&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Nm&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">400</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt90"> </span><span about="#mwt90">N⋅m
(295</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt90"> </span><span about="#mwt90">lbf⋅ft)</span>. The engine
uses two selective catalytic reduction filters with dual AdBlue
injection. The manual transmission is not offered, with the seven-speed
dual-clutch automatic being the only option. The suspension is shared
with the GTI, having
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MacPherson_strut" id="mw5Q"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="MacPherson strut">MacPherson struts</a> up
front and a
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Multi-link_suspension" id="mw5g"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Multi-link suspension">multi-link</a> system at
the rear. 18-inch and 19-inch wheels are available. Plaid upholstery is
standard like on the GTI, but grey highlights are used rather than red;
the steering wheel also has touch-sensitive multi-function controls. An
electronic shift lever is used in place of the patterned shift knob
found on the
GTI.<sup><a href="#cite_note-Motor1_GTD/GTE-20" id="mw5w"><span id="mw6A"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mw6Q"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>20<span id="mw6g"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a><a href="#cite_note-21" id="mw6w"><span id="mw7A"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mw7Q"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>21<span id="mw7g"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup>

In the 2024 model year, Volkswagen discontinued the GTD.

- <div id="mw8Q">

  <div id="mw8g" class="thumb" style="width: 230px; height: 173px;">

  <span id="mw8w" typeof="mw:File"><a

<div id="mw_Q" class="section" mw-section-id="6"
aria-labelledby="Golf_GTI">

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2">

## Golf GTI

<span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\[</span>[edit](/w/index.php?title=Volkswagen_Golf_Mk8&action=edit&section=6 "Edit section: Golf GTI")<span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\]</span></span>

</div>

The Golf GTI is a hot hatchback version that is powered by a 2.0-litre
turbocharged direct-injection petrol engine (TSI) producing
<span id="mw_w" about="#mwt96" typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;180&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;kW&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;PS kW hp&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;order&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;out&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">245</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt96"> </span><span about="#mwt96">PS
(180</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt96"> </span><span about="#mwt96">kW;
241</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt96"> </span><span about="#mwt96">hp)</span> and
<span id="mwAQA" about="#mwt97" typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;370&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Nm&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">370</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt97"> </span><span about="#mwt97">N⋅m
(273</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt97"> </span><span about="#mwt97">lbf⋅ft)</span>. The bodywork
is nearly identical to the GTE, however the GTI is equipped with
different wheels, badges, and red grille accents. The rear bumper has
dual exit exhausts. Vehicle Dynamics Manager allows for more
adjustability of the adaptive suspension dampers, while an Individual
setting joins the other driving modes. The suspension is lowered by
<span id="mwAQE" about="#mwt98" typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;15&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;mm&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;in&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">15</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt98"> </span><span about="#mwt98">mm
(0.6</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt98"> </span><span about="#mwt98">in)</span>. Inside, plaid
upholstery is standard, with additional red accents on the seats and
steering wheel. 17-inch wheels are standard in Europe, with optional
18-inch and 19-inch
wheels.<sup><a href="#cite_note-22" id="mwAQI"><span id="mwAQM"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwAQQ"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>22<span id="mwAQU"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup>

- <div id="mwAQc">

<div id="mwASs" class="section" mw-section-id="7"
aria-labelledby="Golf_GTI_380">

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3">

### Golf GTI 380

<span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\[</span>[edit](/w/index.php?title=Volkswagen_Golf_Mk8&action=edit&section=7 "Edit section: Golf GTI 380")<span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\]</span></span>

</div>

Announced by Volkswagen of America on 30 August 2023, the Golf GTI 380
is a special edition model for the 2024 North American model year. It is
a commemorative model celebrating the Golf GTI's final production year
with a manual transmission in North America. Future model years will
only offer Volkswagen’s DSG
transmission.<sup><a href="#cite_note-23" id="mwAS0"><span id="mwAS4"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwAS8"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>23<span id="mwATA"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup>

</div>

</div>

<div id="mwATE" class="section" mw-section-id="8"
aria-labelledby="Golf_R">

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2">

## Golf R

<span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\[</span>[edit](/w/index.php?title=Volkswagen_Golf_Mk8&action=edit&section=8 "Edit section: Golf R")<span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\]</span></span>

</div>

The Golf R is powered by a 2.0-litre turbocharged direct-injection
petrol engine (TSI) producing <span id="mwATM" about="#mwt106"
typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;235&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;kW&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;PS kW hp&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;order&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;out&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">320</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt106"> </span><span about="#mwt106">PS
(235</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt106"> </span><span about="#mwt106">kW;
315</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt106"> </span><span about="#mwt106">hp)</span> and
<span id="mwATQ" about="#mwt107" typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;420&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Nm&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">420</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt107"> </span><span about="#mwt107">N⋅m
(310</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt107"> </span><span about="#mwt107">lbf⋅ft)</span> which is an
increase of <span id="mwATU" about="#mwt108" typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;15&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;kW&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;PS kW hp&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;order&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;out&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">20</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt108"> </span><span about="#mwt108">PS
(15</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt108"> </span><span about="#mwt108">kW;
20</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt108"> </span><span about="#mwt108">hp)</span> and
<span id="mwATY" about="#mwt109" typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;40&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Nm&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">40</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt109"> </span><span about="#mwt109">N⋅m
(30</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt109"> </span><span about="#mwt109">lbf⋅ft)</span> when
compared to the Mk7. It is offered with either the seven-speed
dual-clutch automatic globally or the six-speed manual (US and Canadian
markets only) in both the hatch and estate body styles. The R is
<span id="mwATc" about="#mwt110" typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;20&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;mm&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;in&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">20</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt110"> </span><span about="#mwt110">mm
(0.8</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt110"> </span><span about="#mwt110">in)</span> lower than the
standard Golf, and has a stiffer suspension incorporating an aluminium
front subframe. The all-wheel-drive system has been updated and benefits
from a torque-vectoring rear differential. Dynamic Chassis Control has
also been updated to work with the Vehicle Dynamics Manager, allowing
for a
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Drifting_(motorsport)" id="mwATg"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Drifting (motorsport)">Drift</a> Mode function.
The exterior features quad exhaust tips and 19-inch wheels, while the
interior is similar to the GTI, and has Nappa leather bucket seats as
well as several R badges and an R-specific driver's
display.<sup><a href="#cite_note-24" id="mwATk"><span id="mwATo"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwATs"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>24<span id="mwATw"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a><a href="#cite_note-25" id="mwAT0"><span id="mwAT4"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwAT8"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>25<span id="mwAUA"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup>

- <div id="mwAUI">

  <div id="mwAUM" class="thumb" style="width: 230px; height: 143px;">

<div id="mwAWA" class="section" mw-section-id="9"
aria-labelledby="Golf_R_333">

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3">

### Golf R 333

<span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\[</span>[edit](/w/index.php?title=Volkswagen_Golf_Mk8&action=edit&section=9 "Edit section: Golf R 333")<span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\]</span></span>

</div>

Debuted on 31 May 2023, the limited edition Golf R 333 is meant to
replace the 2022 Golf R "20 Years
Edition".<sup><a href="#cite_note-26" id="mwAWI"><span id="mwAWM"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwAWQ"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>26<span id="mwAWU"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a><a href="#cite_note-27" id="mwAWY"><span id="mwAWc"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwAWg"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>27<span id="mwAWk"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup>

- <div id="mwAWs">

<div id="mwAXc" class="section" mw-section-id="10"
aria-labelledby="Facelift">

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2">

## <span id="mwAXg" class="anchor" x-id="Facelift"></span><span id="A8_facelift" class="anchor"></span><span id="Mk8.5" class="anchor"></span><span id="2024" class="anchor"></span> Facelift

<span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\[</span>[edit](/w/index.php?title=Volkswagen_Golf_Mk8&action=edit&section=10 "Edit section:  Facelift")<span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\]</span></span>

</div>

In January 2024, the Golf range was given a mild facelift, known as the
**Golf Mk8.5**.<sup><a href="#cite_note-28" id="mwAXs"><span id="mwAXw"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwAX0"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>28<span id="mwAX4"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup> Changes include
redesigned headlights with hexagonal-shaped, daytime running lights and
a new option of an IQ.Light LED matrix setup, a new illuminated logo,
new bumpers, new taillights with three selectable patterns, new exterior
colours, new alloy wheel designs, a new MIB4 software for the
infotainment system, touch-slider controls for <a
href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Heating,_ventilation,_and_air_conditioning"
id="mwAX8" rel="mw:WikiLink"
title="Heating, ventilation, and air conditioning">HVAC controls</a> are
illuminated, the most expensive Golf variants revert to conventional
buttons on the multi-functional steering wheel, a few new <a
href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Advanced_driver-assistance_system"
id="mwAYA" rel="mw:WikiLink"
title="Advanced driver-assistance system">driver assistance system</a>
features and updated standard features across trim
levels.<sup><a href="#cite_note-29" id="mwAYE"><span id="mwAYI"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwAYM"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>29<span id="mwAYQ"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup> The facelift continues to
be offered in both hatchback and Variant (wagon) body
styles.<sup><a href="#cite_note-:8-30" id="mwAYU"><span id="mwAYY"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwAYc"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>30<span id="mwAYg"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup>

The most significant interior update is the introduction of the MIB4
infotainment system, featuring a faster processor and available in
10.4-inch or 12.9-inch floating touchscreen
configurations.<sup><a href="#cite_note-:8-30" id="mwAYo"><span id="mwAYs"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwAYw"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>30<span id="mwAY0"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup> Volkswagen replaced the
controversial touch-sensitive steering wheel controls with physical
buttons in response to customer
feedback.<sup><a href="#cite_note-31" id="mwAY4"><span id="mwAY8"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwAZA"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>31<span id="mwAZE"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup> The touch sliders
controlling cabin temperature are now illuminated for improved nighttime
visibility. The system integrates ChatGPT into the IDA voice assistant,
though this feature has received mixed reviews.

The powertrain lineup includes four turbocharged petrol engines (TSI),
two turbodiesel engines (TDI), two 48-volt mild hybrid turbocharged
petrol engines (eTSI), and two plug-in hybrid petrol systems (eHybrid
and GTE). At launch, the 1.5-liter TSI engine is available with either
115 or 150 PS paired with a six-speed manual transmission, while the 1.5
eTSI mild hybrid variants offer the same power outputs exclusively with
a seven-speed dual-clutch transmission. The updated eHybrid and GTE
plug-in hybrid models feature a larger 1.5-liter TSI engine replacing
the previous 1.4-liter unit, paired with a 19.7<span id="mwAZM"
typeof="mw:Entity"> </span>kWh battery providing up to 82 miles of
electric range. Manual transmission options were discontinued globally
for the 2025 model year, with DSG dual-clutch transmissions becoming
standard across the range.

The Golf R variant received a power increase for the Mk8.5 update, with
the 2.0-liter EA888 turbocharged four-cylinder engine now producing 333
PS and 310 lb-ft of torque, representing a 14 PS increase over the
previous model. Acceleration from 0-62 mph is achieved in 4.6 seconds
for the hatchback and 4.8 seconds for the Variant wagon, with power
delivered through a seven-speed DSG transmission and Volkswagen's
4Motion all-wheel drive system. The GTI Clubsport, Golf R, and Golf
Variant R received the facelift treatment in the second half of 2024.

<div id="mwAbQ" class="section" mw-section-id="11"
aria-labelledby="Powertrain">

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2">

## Powertrain

<span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\[</span>[edit](/w/index.php?title=Volkswagen_Golf_Mk8&action=edit&section=11 "Edit section: Powertrain")<span class="mw-editsection-bracket">\]</span></span>

</div>

All internal combustion engines are turbocharged three- or four-cylinder
units;<sup><a href="#cite_note-32" id="mwAbY"><span id="mwAbc"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwAbg"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>32<span id="mwAbk"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup> engine options include
petrol, mild-hybrid, plug-in hybrid, diesel and natural gas powertrains.
The previous e-Golf model is no longer available, as it was replaced by
the <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Volkswagen_ID.3" id="mwAbo"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Volkswagen ID.3">ID.3</a>.

**TSI**

href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European_emission_standards#Toxic_emission:_stages_and_legal_framework"
id="mwAcE" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="European emission standards">Euro
6d</a> compliant countries consist of a 1.0-litre turbocharged petrol
engine (TSI) with <span id="mwAcI" about="#mwt142"

<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Variable_displacement" id="mwAcg"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Variable displacement">Active Cylinder
Management</a>. A
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Compressed_natural_gas"
id="mwAck" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Compressed natural gas">compressed

the 1.5-litre turbocharged petrol engine only with either

<sup><a href="#cite_note-2024presspack-33" id="mwAcw"><span id="mwAc0"

class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup>

***Euro 5***

TSI models sold in Euro 5 compliant countries, such as Australia, will
at launch feature one option, a 1.4-litre turbocharged petrol engine
(TSI) with <span id="mwAdQ" about="#mwt149" typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;110&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;kW&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;PS kW hp&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;order&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;out&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">150</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt149"> </span><span about="#mwt149">PS
(110</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt149"> </span><span about="#mwt149">kW;
148</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt149"> </span><span about="#mwt149">hp)</span>. Unlike
previous Golfs which featured a 7-speed dual-clutch gearbox (DSG), this
engine will be paired with a traditional 8-speed
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Torque_converter" id="mwAdU"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Torque converter">torque converter</a>
automatic. The transmission choice is due in part to the specific engine
calibration (and automatic transmission combination) developed to meet
Australia's outdated Euro 5 emissions regulations introduced in 2009,
which are about a decade behind European
standards.<sup><a href="#cite_note-34" id="mwAdY"><span id="mwAdc"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwAdg"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>34<span id="mwAdk"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup>

**eTSI** eTSI models use the same engines as the TSI models with the
addition of a mild-hybrid system and a powerful brake energy
recuperation function, paired exclusively with the 7-speed dual-clutch
gearbox (DSG). The energy stored in the 48<span id="mwAdw"
typeof="mw:Entity"> </span>V lithium-ion battery supplies the
12<span id="mwAd0" typeof="mw:Entity"> </span>V vehicle electrical
system and drives the 48<span id="mwAd4" typeof="mw:Entity"> </span>V
belt starter generator. The engines produce <span id="mwAd8"
about="#mwt153" typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;81&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;kW&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;PS kW hp&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;order&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;out&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">110</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt153"> </span><span about="#mwt153">PS
(81</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt153"> </span><span about="#mwt153">kW;
109</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt153"> </span><span about="#mwt153">hp)</span>,
<span id="mwAeA" about="#mwt154" typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;130&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;PS&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;PS kW hp&quot;},&quot;order&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;out&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">130</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt154"> </span><span about="#mwt154">PS
(96</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt154"> </span><span about="#mwt154">kW;
130</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt154"> </span><span about="#mwt154">hp)</span> or
<span id="mwAeE" about="#mwt155" typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;110&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;kW&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;PS kW hp&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;order&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;out&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">150</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt155"> </span><span about="#mwt155">PS
(110</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt155"> </span><span about="#mwt155">kW;
148</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt155"> </span><span about="#mwt155">hp)</span>.

**eHybrid** eHybrid models use a 1.4-litre turbocharged plug-in hybrid
engine supplemented by a 13
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kilowatt_hour" id="mwAeQ"
class="mw-redirect" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Kilowatt hour">kWh</a>
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lithium_ion_battery" id="mwAeU"
class="mw-redirect" rel="mw:WikiLink"
title="Lithium ion battery">lithium ion battery</a>, with a 6-speed DSG.
The
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/All-electric_range" id="mwAeY"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="All-electric range">all-electric range</a> is
rated at about <span id="mwAec" about="#mwt156" typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;60&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;km&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;mi&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;off&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">60
kilometres (37 miles)</span> in EV mode. The engine produces
<span id="mwAeg" about="#mwt157" typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;150&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;kW&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;PS kW hp&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;order&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;out&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">204</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt157"> </span><span about="#mwt157">PS
(150</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt157"> </span><span about="#mwt157">kW;
201</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt157"> </span><span about="#mwt157">hp)</span> or
<span id="mwAek" about="#mwt158" typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;180&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;kW&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;PS kW hp&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;order&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;out&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">245</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt158"> </span><span about="#mwt158">PS
(180</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt158"> </span><span about="#mwt158">kW;
241</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt158"> </span><span about="#mwt158">hp)</span>.

**TDI** <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/TDI_(engine)" id="mwAew"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="TDI (engine)">TDI</a> models utilise a new twin
dosing system featuring dual
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diesel_exhaust_fluid" id="mwAe0"
rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Diesel exhaust fluid">AdBlue</a>
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Selective_catalytic_reduction"
id="mwAe4" rel="mw:WikiLink"
title="Selective catalytic reduction">selective catalytic reduction</a>,
which lowers nitrogen oxide emissions (NOx) by up to 80% compared to the
Mk7.<sup><a href="#cite_note-35" id="mwAe8"><span id="mwAfA"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwAfE"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>35<span id="mwAfI"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup> A 2.0-litre turbocharged
diesel unit is used, producing <span id="mwAfM" about="#mwt159"
typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;115&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;PS&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;PS kW hp&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;order&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;out&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">115</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt159"> </span><span about="#mwt159">PS
(85</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt159"> </span><span about="#mwt159">kW;
113</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt159"> </span><span about="#mwt159">hp)</span> or
<span id="mwAfQ" about="#mwt160" typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;110&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;kW&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;PS kW hp&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;order&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;out&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">150</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt160"> </span><span about="#mwt160">PS
(110</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt160"> </span><span about="#mwt160">kW;
148</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt160"> </span><span about="#mwt160">hp)</span>.<sup><a href="#cite_note-36" id="mwAfU"><span id="mwAfY"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwAfc"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>36<span id="mwAfg"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a><a href="#cite_note-37" id="mwAfk"><span id="mwAfo"
class="mw-reflink-text"><span id="mwAfs"
class="cite-bracket">[</span>37<span id="mwAfw"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup> Both engines were
originally available with 6 speed manual or 7 speed automatic gear boxes
depending on trim level. In 2024 for the Golf '8.5' the manual gearbox
was restricted to the <span id="mwAf0" about="#mwt161"
typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;115&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;PS&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;PS kW hp&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;order&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;out&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">115</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt161"> </span><span about="#mwt161">PS
(85</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt161"> </span><span about="#mwt161">kW;
113</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt161"> </span><span about="#mwt161">hp)</span> and the
automatic transmission to the <span id="mwAf4" about="#mwt162"
typeof="mw:Transclusion"
mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;convert&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Convert&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;110&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;kW&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;PS kW hp&quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;0&quot;},&quot;order&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;out&quot;},&quot;abbr&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;on&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">150</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"
about="#mwt162"> </span><span about="#mwt162">PS
(110</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"

148</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"

**Manual** transmission options were not available in the Australian
market. The last manual transmission offered for the Golf in Australia
was in the Mk7.5 MY19.

id="mwA7k" rel="mw:WikiLink"
title="Insurance Institute for Highway Safety">IIHS</a> and received a

award:<sup><a href="#cite_note-44" id="mwA7s"><span id="mwA7w"

class="cite-bracket">[</span>44<span id="mwA74"
class="cite-bracket">]</span></span></a></sup>

|                                                     |            |

| Small overlap front (Driver)                        | Good       |
| Small overlap front (Passenger)                     | Good       |
| Moderate overlap front                              | Good       |
| Side (original test)                                | Good       |
| Roof strength                                       | Good       |
| Head restraints and seats                           | Good       |
| Headlights                                          | Acceptable |
| Front crash prevention (Vehicle-to-Vehicle)         | Superior   |
| Front crash prevention (Vehicle-to-Pedestrian, day) | Advanced   |
|                                                     |            |

href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Insurance_Institute_for_Highway_Safety"
